Great. Now let's also double check the arrow value clause in the final use: V(Arrow T1 T2) rho [R0] w where w is a value. The clause is: ∀a, R(T1) a → R(T2)(app w a). Note: we don't require a to be a value; R(T1) a gives a reduction to a value a' with V(T1)(a'), and V(BVar 0) = R0; the arrow clause gives R(T2)(app w a) directly as the definition (since we're using the *value* interpretation V on w and the relation R on the result). Wait, the arrow clause in V is defined as `forall a, R T1 a -> R T2 (app t a)` — so there's no need to reduce a; the definition handles arbitrary a. But then in the Abs case's proof of V(Arrow)(abs ...), we need to show ∀a, R(T1) a → R(T2)(app (abs) a) — using a's reduction to a value ✓. Good, that's consistent.

So the final application: R(BVar 0)(v) ✓ via R0 v = v = v, then R(BVar 0)(app w v) = ∃u, app w v -->* u ∧ value u ∧ u = v ✓ → app w v -->* v ✓.

Now, let's also verify the Abs case proof's use of the arrow clause: the goal is V(Arrow T1 T2)(tm_abs (σT1) (subst t2)) = ∀a, R(T1) a → R(T2)(app (abs ...) a). We have R(T1) a = ∃a', multi a a' ∧ value a' ∧ V(T1)(a'). Then app (abs) a -->* app (abs) a' (multi_app_r requires the function to be a value: `multi_app_r: value v1 -> t2 -->* t2' -> app v1 t2 -->* app v1 t2'`, using ST_App2 which requires value v1 ✓ — we have value (tm_abs (σT1)(subst t2)) from the goal's setup). Then app (abs) a' --> open_tm (subst t2) a' (ST_AppAbs with lc (abs ...) and value a' ✓). The IH gives R(T2)(open_tm (subst t2) a') (after commutation). So app (abs) a -->* ... -->* w by trans with the IH's multi ✓.

Now let's also double-check the T_App case of the fundamental theorem:
Given has_type Δ Γ (tm_app t1 t2) T2 with premises t1 : T1→T2, t2 : T1.
Goal: R rho [] T2 (subst_all (app t1 t2)) = R(T2)(app s1 s2) where s_i = subst_all t_i; plus lc conclusions.
IH1: R rho [] (Arrow T1 T2) s1 ∧ lc_ty T1→T2 ∧ lc_tm s1.
IH2: R rho [] T1 s2 ∧ lc_ty T1 ∧ lc_tm s2.
R(Arrow T1 T2)(s1) = ∃v1, s1 -->* v1 ∧ value v1 ∧ V(Arrow T1 T2)(v1).
V(Arrow)(v1) = ∀a, R(T1) a → R(T2)(app v1 a).
We need R(T2)(app s1 s2). Since s1 -->* v1, we have app s1 s2 -->* app v1 s2 (multi_app_l with lc s2 ✓ from IH2). Then apply V(Arrow) with a := s2: need R(T1)(s2) ✓ from IH2. Get R(T2)(app v1 s2) = ∃w, app v1 s2 -->* w ∧ ... So app s1 s2 -->* w by trans ✓. So R(T2)(app s1 s2) ✓.
lc conclusions: lc_ty_at 0 T2 from IH1's lc of (Arrow T1 T2) → inversion ✓; lc_tm_at 0 0 (app s1 s2) from IH1/IH2 lc ✓.

T_Var: Γ lookup gives T; env gives R(T)(σ_tm x) and lc; the term subst_all (fvar x) = σ_tm x ✓; lc_ty_at 0 T from wf_ty Δ T → lc ✓.

T_Choice: Goal R(T)(choice s1 s2). IH1: R(T)(s1) = ∃v, s1 -->* v ∧ value v ∧ V(T)(v); then choice s1 s2 --> s1 (ST_ChoiceLeft with lc s1, lc s2 ✓ from IHs) -->* v ✓. Similarly we can just use IH1 (either branch). ✓.

T_Abs: as above.

Now the big question: **is the fundamental theorem's statement actually provable with these relations?** Let's scrutinize the tricky parts:

1. The All clause quantifies over arbitrary R0 (a predicate on terms, no value restriction). In the TAbs case, we prove R(T1 with R0::[])(tapp (tabs s') U) for all U lc, R0. 2. The Abs case: V(Arrow T1 T2)(abs) proven for arbitrary R(T1) argument a. The proof uses the IH at the premise with env extended x↦a' where V(T1)(a') and value a'. The proof for the abstraction uses multi_app_r with value (abs) ✓, ST_AppAbs with value a' ✓, and the IH's R(T2)(open_tm ...) which includes its own multi to a value ✓. So R(T2)(app (abs) a) holds with witness w from the IH and the concatenated multi ✓.

4. The T_App case: uses multi_app_l with lc s2 ✓.

V(U) interprets U's free atoms via rho; V(σ_ty U) interprets σ_ty U's free atoms via rho. Since σ_ty substitutes atoms by types, and rho interprets atoms by predicates, these differ in general.

Hmm! Wait, but there's a subtlety: the goal term is `tapp s1 (σ_ty U)` with the type `open_ty T1 U` in the source. Let me reconsider the whole setup. In the standard binary parametricity with type substitutions σ1, σ2, the relation ⟦T⟧_ρ is defined on the *source* type T with the environment ρ mapping each free var X to a relation R_X that is contained in ⟦σ1 X⟧ × ⟦σ2 X⟧. The soundness lemma then gives ⟦T⟧_ρ ⊆ ⟦σ1 T⟧ × ⟦σ2 T⟧. Here the "relation at X" is arbitrary but *sound* w.r.t. the substitution types. I think the design is sound. Now let's think about the *proof of the fundamental theorem* structure and the lemmas needed:

For each typing rule, we prove:
(a) R rho [] T (subst_all σ_ty σ_tm t)
(b) lc_ty_at 0 T
(c) lc_tm_at 0 0 (subst_all σ_ty σ_tm t)

with hypotheses:
- Hσ : ∀X, lc_ty_at 0 (σ_ty X)
- Henv : ∀x V, lookup_context x Γ = Some V → R rho [] V (σ_tm x) ∧ lc_tm_at 0 0 (σ_tm x)
- (maybe Hwf : ∀x V, lookup_context x Γ = Some V → wf_ty Δ V, for (b) at T_Var)
- Δ and the derivation.

The commutation lemma `subst_all σ_ty σ_tm' (open_tm t (fvar x)) = open_tm (subst_all σ_ty σ_tm t) a` where σ_tm' = σ_tm[x↦a]: here x is a term variable; the term substitution replaces free term atoms x; the type substitution σ_ty replaces type atoms. Hmm wait, where does lc_ty_at 0 U come in? In the BVar case i=k, LHS V(U) (under env pre++xi); RHS lookup k (pre ++ V(U)::xi) = V(U) — the same predicate V rho xi U? In the RHS I wrote V rho xi U; but the LHS's V of U is under the env pre++xi. If U is lc at 0, V rho (pre++xi) U = V rho xi U (env irrelevance), so we need the hypothesis lc_ty_at 0 U ✓. And in the All case, the IH pushes pre' = Q::pre and keeps xi: RHS env pre' ++ V rho xi U :: xi = Q :: pre ++ V(U) :: xi ✓. And the goal's RHS env is pre ++ V(U) :: xi under the All binder Q: Q :: pre ++ V(U) :: xi ✓. Match. But the LHS env for the IH: pre' ++ xi = Q :: pre ++ xi ✓ matches the goal's LHS under the All binder ✓.

So the lemma needs both lc_ty_at (S k) T and lc_ty_at 0 U. Also, the RHS uses V rho xi U regardless of pre; with U lc, V rho (pre++xi) U = V rho xi U ✓ (needed in the BVar i=k case: LHS = V rho (pre++xi) U, RHS = V rho xi U).

Let me double check the All case more carefully:
LHS: V rho (pre++xi) (open_ty_rec k U (Ty_All T1)) = V rho (pre++xi) (Ty_All (open_ty_rec (S k) U T1)) = ∀Q, V rho (Q :: pre ++ xi) (open_ty_rec (S k) U T1).
RHS: V rho (pre ++ V(U) :: xi) (Ty_All T1) = ∀Q, V rho (Q :: pre ++ V(U) :: xi) T1.
IH at k' = S k, pre' = Q :: pre, same xi, same U:

  length (Q::pre) = S k ✓,
  gives: V rho (Q::pre ++ xi) (open_ty_rec (S k) U T1) ↔ V rho (Q::pre ++ V(U)::xi) T1.
The RHS env in the IH is (Q::pre) ++ V(U) :: xi = Q :: (pre ++ V(U) :: xi) ✓ matches the goal's RHS ✓.
So the lemma holds for k with pre of length k, and the All case applies it at k+1 with pre extended ✓. Wait, but there's a mismatch: the IH statement uses `V rho xi U` in the RHS, and in the All case the RHS env is Q::pre ++ V rho xi U :: xi. The goal's RHS is Q :: pre ++ V rho xi U :: xi ✓ same. ```

Now, an important detail: `lookup_level` and `nth`. Let me define:
```
Fixpoint lookup_level (xi : list sem) (i : nat) : sem :=
  match xi with
  | [] => fun _ => False
  | P :: xi' => match i with 0 => P | S j => lookup_level xi' j end
  end.
```
Then lemmas:
- lookup_level_0: lookup_level (P :: xi) 0 = P (definitional).
- lookup_level_S: lookup_level (P :: xi) (S j) = lookup_level xi j (definitional).
- lookup_level_app_lt: forall xi1 xi2 i, i < length xi1 -> lookup_level (xi1 ++ xi2) i = lookup_level xi1 i.
- lookup_level_app_ge: forall xi1 xi2 i, length xi1 <= i -> lookup_level (xi1 ++ xi2) i = lookup_level xi2 (i - length xi1).
- lookup_level_app_eq: lookup_level (pre ++ P :: xi) (length pre) = P. (follows from ge with i - length pre = 0)
Proofs by induction on xi1 / i.

In the BVar i=k case: i = k = length pre, so lookup_level (pre ++ V(U) :: xi) k = lookup_level (V(U)::xi) (k - length pre) = lookup_level (V(U)::xi) 0 = V(U) ✓.

In the i<k case: lookup_level (pre++xi) i = lookup_level pre i; lookup_level (pre ++ V(U)::xi) i = lookup_level pre i (since i < length pre) ✓.

Hmm, but with the lc hypothesis lc_ty_at (S k) T, in the BVar case we have i ≤ k, so either i < k or i = k ✓ (no i > k). In Coq, I'll do case analysis on `i < k` or `i = k` or `k < i`; the third case is excluded by lc.

Let me write the first chunk now.

```coq
(* ---------- basic utilities ---------- *)

Fixpoint max_list (l : list nat) : nat :=
  match l with
  | [] => 0
  | x :: l' => Nat.max x (max_list l')
  end.

Definition fresh (l : list nat) : nat := S (max_list l).

Lemma max_list_ge : forall l x, In x l -> x <= max_list l.
Proof.
  induction l as [| y l IH]; intros x Hin.
  - contradiction.
  - simpl. destruct Hin as [<- | Hin].
    + apply Nat.le_max_l.
    + eapply Nat.le_trans; [apply IH; exact Hin | apply Nat.le_max_r].
Qed.

Lemma fresh_not_in : forall l, ~ In (fresh l) l.
Proof.
  intros l Hin. unfold fresh in Hin.
  pose proof (max_list_ge l (S (max_list l)) Hin). lia.
Qed.
```
Wait, max_list_ge with x = S(max_list l) gives S(max_list l) ≤ max_list l, lia ✓.

fv functions:
```coq
Fixpoint fv_ty (T : ty) : list atom :=
  match T with
  | Ty_BVar _ => []
  | Ty_FVar X => [X]
  | Ty_Arrow T1 T2 => fv_ty T1 ++ fv_ty T2
  | Ty_All T1 => fv_ty T1
  end.

Fixpoint fv_tm_ty (t : tm) : list atom :=
  match t with
  | tm_bvar _ => []
  | tm_fvar _ => []
  | tm_abs T t1 => fv_ty T ++ fv_tm_ty t1
  | tm_app t1 t2 => fv_tm_ty t1 ++ fv_tm_ty t2
  | tm_tabs t1 => fv_tm_ty t1
  | tm_tapp t1 T => fv_tm_ty t1 ++ fv_ty T
  | tm_choice t1 t2 => fv_tm_ty t1 ++ fv_tm_ty t2
  end.

Fixpoint fv_tm (t : tm) : list atom :=
  match t with
  | tm_bvar _ => []
  | tm_fvar x => [x]
  | tm_abs _ t1 => fv_tm t1
  | tm_app t1 t2 => fv_tm t1 ++ fv_tm t2
  | tm_tabs t1 => fv_tm t1
  | tm_tapp t1 _ => fv_tm t1
  | tm_choice t1 t2 => fv_tm t1 ++ fv_tm t2
  end.

Fixpoint fv_ctx (G : context) : list atom :=
  match G with
  | [] => []
  | (x, T) :: G' => x :: fv_ty T ++ fv_ctx G'
  end.
```
Note `(x, T) :: G'` pattern syntax in a Fixpoint: Coq allows `(x, T) :: G'` as a pattern for lists of pairs ✓.

Lemma lc_ty_at_mono : forall k k' T, k <= k' -> lc_ty_at k T -> lc_ty_at k' T.

  intros k k' T Hle H. revert k' Hle.
  induction H; intros k' Hle.
  - apply lc_ty_bvar. lia.
  - apply lc_ty_fvar.
  - apply lc_ty_arrow; [apply IHlc_ty_at1 | apply IHlc_ty_at2]; auto with arith.
  - apply lc_ty_all. apply IHlc_ty_at. lia.
Qed.

Lemma lc_ty_at_open : forall k T U,

  - FVar: lc_ty_fvar.
  - Arrow: lc_ty_arrow; [apply IHT1 | apply IHT2]; [inversion Hlc | inversion Hlc] ...
  - All: simpl gives Ty_All (open_ty_rec (S k) U T1). Need lc_ty_at k (Ty_All ...) = lc_ty_at (S k) (open_ty_rec (S k) U T1). Apply IHT1 with k := S k and U := U; need lc_ty_at (S (S k)) T1 from lc_ty_at (S k) (Ty_All T1) ✓ and lc_ty_at (S k) U from HU by mono ✓.
✓.

Inverse:
```
Lemma lc_ty_at_open_inv : forall k T U,
  lc_ty_at k (open_ty_rec k U T) -> lc_ty_at (S k) T.
```
Proof by induction on T (generalizing k):
  - BVar i: simpl; destruct (Nat.eqb k i) eqn:E.
    + (* i = k *) apply Nat.eqb_eq in E; subst i; apply lc_ty_bvar; lia.
    + (* i ≠ k *) apply Nat.eqb_neq in E. Inversion the premise: lc_ty_at k (Ty_BVar i) → i < k → i < S k ✓.
  - FVar ✓.
  - Arrow: inversion premise; apply lc_ty_arrow with IHT1, IHT2 ✓.
  - All: premise lc_ty_at k (Ty_All (open_ty_rec (S k) U T1)) → lc_ty_at (S k) (open_ty_rec (S k) U T1); apply IHT1 with k := S k → lc_ty_at (S (S k)) T1 → lc_ty_all ✓.
✓.

Note: in the BVar case, `simpl` might not unfold `open_ty_rec` if k is a variable? `open_ty_rec k U (Ty_BVar i)` reduces by the Fixpoint's match on T = Ty_BVar i, giving the if-then-else ✓ (k is a variable, but the match is on the third argument which is a constructor) ✓.

Hmm, careful: open_ty_rec's arguments: (k : nat) (U T : ty). The Fixpoint decreases on T (the third argument) ✓. So `simpl` should reduce ✓.

Also, in the All case, the recursive call open_ty_rec (S k) U T1: the induction is on T, and we need the IH generalized over k; `induction T` gives IH for T1 and T2 with k universally quantified if we do `intros k T U` with k after T? Better: `induction T; intros k U H` where the IH is `forall k U, lc_ty_at k (open_ty_rec k U T1) -> lc_ty_at (S k) T1` ✓ if k is introduced after T in the induction. Let me set the lemma statement as `forall T k U, ...` and do `induction T; intros k U H`.

Now wf_ty→lc_ty_at 0:
```
Lemma wf_ty_lc : forall Delta T, wf_ty Delta T -> lc_ty_at 0 T.
Proof.
  intros Delta T H. induction H.
  - apply lc_ty_fvar.
  - apply lc_ty_arrow; assumption.
  - (* WF_All *) apply lc_ty_all. 
    (* H : forall X, ~ In X L -> wf_ty (X :: Delta) (open_ty T (Ty_FVar X)) *)
    (* IH : forall X, ~ In X L -> lc_ty_at 0 (open_ty T (Ty_FVar X)) *)
    pick X fresh from L: e.g., X := fresh L.
    have Hnotin : ~ In (fresh L) L := fresh_not_in L.
    have := IH (fresh L) Hnotin : lc_ty_at 0 (open_ty T (Ty_FVar (fresh L))).
    open_ty T X = open_ty_rec 0 (Ty_FVar X) T.
    apply lc_ty_at_open_inv with (k := 0) (U := Ty_FVar (fresh L)) → lc_ty_at 1 T ✓.
Qed.
```
Note the induction on wf_ty: the WF_All constructor's IH is exactly `(forall X, ~ In X L -> lc_ty_at 0 (open_ty T (Ty_FVar X)))` ✓.

2. Unfold R_t: destruct to get v0, Hmulti_t, Hvalue_v0, HV.

5. From HV_arrow v: need R (BVar 0) v. Provide witness v: multi_refl v, value v, R0 v = eq_refl.

7. multi_trans with app w v -->* v ✓.

```coq
(* ============================================================ *)
(* Auxiliary infrastructure                                     *)
(* ============================================================ *)

Fixpoint max_list (l : list nat) : nat :=
  match l with
  | [] => 0
  | x :: l' => Nat.max x (max_list l')
  end.

Definition fresh (l : list nat) : nat := S (max_list l).

Lemma max_list_ge : forall l x, In x l -> x <= max_list l.
Proof.
  induction l as [| y l IH]; intros x Hin.
  - destruct Hin.
  - simpl in *. destruct Hin as [<- | Hin].
    + apply Nat.le_max_l.
    + eapply Nat.le_trans; eauto. apply Nat.le_max_r.
Qed.

Lemma fresh_not_in : forall l, ~ In (fresh l) l.
Proof.
  intros l Hin. unfold fresh in Hin.
  pose proof (max_list_ge l (S (max_list l)) Hin). lia.
Qed.
```

Type substitution for terms:
```
Fixpoint ty_subst_all (sigma : atom -> ty) (T : ty) : ty :=
  match T with
  | Ty_BVar i => Ty_BVar i
  | Ty_FVar X => sigma X
  | Ty_Arrow T1 T2 => Ty_Arrow (ty_subst_all sigma T1) (ty_subst_all sigma T2)
  | Ty_All T1 => Ty_All (ty_subst_all sigma T1)
  end.

Lemma lc_ty_at_ty_subst_all : forall T sigma K,
  lc_ty_at K T ->
  (forall X, lc_ty_at 0 (sigma X)) ->
  lc_ty_at K (ty_subst_all sigma T).
Proof.

  - apply lc_ty_at_mono with (k := 0); [lia | apply Hsig].

Qed.
```

```
Lemma subst_all_ext : forall t sigma_ty1 sigma_ty2 sigma_tm1 sigma_tm2,
  (forall X, In X (fv_tm_ty t) -> sigma_ty1 X = sigma_ty2 X) ->
  (forall x, In x (fv_tm t) -> sigma_tm1 x = sigma_tm2 x) ->
  subst_all sigma_ty1 sigma_tm1 t = subst_all sigma_ty2 sigma_tm2 t.
Proof.
  induction t as [n | y | T t1 IH1 | t1 IH1 t2 IH2 | t1 IH1 | t1 IH1 T | t1 IH1 t2 IH2];
  intros sty1 sty2 stm1 stm2 Hty Htm; simpl.
  - reflexivity.
  - apply Htm. simpl. left. reflexivity.
  - f_equal.
    + apply ty_subst_all_ext. intros X Hin. apply Hty.
      simpl. apply in_or_app. left. assumption.
    + apply IH1.
      * intros X Hin. apply Hty. simpl. apply in_or_app. right. assumption.
      * intros x Hin. apply Htm. simpl. assumption.
  - f_equal.
    + apply IH1.
      * intros X Hin. apply Hty. simpl. apply in_or_app. left. assumption.
      * intros x Hin. apply Htm. simpl. apply in_or_app. left. assumption.
    + apply IH2.
      * intros X Hin. apply Hty. simpl. apply in_or_app. right. assumption.
      * intros x Hin. apply Htm. simpl. apply in_or_app. right. assumption.
  - f_equal. apply IH1.
    * intros X Hin. apply Hty. simpl. assumption.
    * intros x Hin. apply Htm. simpl. assumption.
  - f_equal.
    + apply IH1.
      * intros X Hin. apply Hty. simpl. apply in_or_app. left. assumption.
      * intros x Hin. apply Htm. simpl. assumption.
    + apply ty_subst_all_ext. intros X Hin. apply Hty.
      simpl. apply in_or_app. right. assumption.
  - f_equal.
    + apply IH1.
      * intros X Hin. apply Hty. simpl. apply in_or_app. left. assumption.
      * intros x Hin. apply Htm. simpl. apply in_or_app. left. assumption.
    + apply IH2.
      * intros X Hin. apply Hty. simpl. apply in_or_app. right. assumption.
      * intros x Hin. apply Htm. simpl. apply in_or_app. right. assumption.
Qed.
```

So open_ty_rec j (sigma X) (sigma a) needs (sigma a) to be syntactically Ty_FVar ... to reduce. Since sigma a is an arbitrary type, it doesn't reduce. But conceptually, the RHS equals sigma a only if open_ty_rec j (sigma X) (sigma a) = sigma a, which is only true when sigma a has no BVar j — e.g., if sigma a is locally closed. In general, the lemma `ty_subst_all sigma (open_ty_rec j (FVar X) T) = open_ty_rec j (sigma X) (ty_subst_all sigma T)` is FALSE when sigma maps atoms to types containing free BVars: e.g., sigma a = Ty_BVar j. Then LHS: ty_subst_all sigma (Ty_FVar a) = Ty_BVar j. RHS: open_ty_rec j (sigma X) (Ty_BVar j) = sigma X. Not equal!

So the commutation lemma requires that the σ types are locally closed (no free BVars) — which is exactly our hypothesis ∀X, lc_ty_at 0 (sigma X). For the proofs of the lc-id lemmas, induction on T/t with explicit patterns; generalize appropriately.

open_ty_rec_lc_id:
```
Lemma open_ty_rec_lc_id : forall T n j U, lc_ty_at n T -> n <= j -> open_ty_rec j U T = T.
Proof.
  induction T; intros n j U Hlc Hle; simpl.
  - inversion Hlc; subst. destruct (Nat.eqb j n0) eqn:E.
    + apply Nat.eqb_eq in E. lia.
    + reflexivity.
  - reflexivity.
  - inversion Hlc; subst. f_equal; [apply IHT1 | apply IHT2]; assumption.
  - inversion Hlc; subst. f_equal. apply IHT with (n := S n); [assumption | lia].
Qed.
```
Careful: the BVar constructor is `Ty_BVar : nat -> ty`; in the induction case the nat is named n0 or similar. I'll use explicit `as` patterns for ty too: `induction T as [i | X | T1 IH1 T2 IH2 | T1 IH1]`.

open_tm_rec_lc_id:
```
Lemma open_tm_rec_lc_id : forall t K k j u, lc_tm_at K k t -> k <= j -> open_tm_rec j u t = t.
Proof.
  induction t as [n | x | T t1 IH1 | t1 IH1 t2 IH2 | t1 IH1 | t1 IH1 T | t1 IH1 t2 IH2];
  intros K k j u Hlc Hle; simpl.
  - inversion Hlc; subst. destruct (Nat.eqb j n) eqn:E.
    + apply Nat.eqb_eq in E. lia.
    + reflexivity.
  - reflexivity.
  - inversion Hlc; subst. f_equal. apply IH1 with (k := S k); [assumption | lia].
  - inversion Hlc; subst. f_equal; [apply IH1 with (k:=k) | apply IH2 with (k:=k)]; assumption.
  - inversion Hlc; subst. f_equal. apply IH1 with (K := S K); [assumption | assumption].
  - inversion Hlc; subst. f_equal; [apply IH1 with (k:=k) | reflexivity]; assumption.
  - inversion Hlc; subst. f_equal; [apply IH1 with (k:=k) | apply IH2 with (k:=k)]; assumption.
Qed.
```
Hmm, under tabs, open_tm_rec keeps k (since it's the term-binder depth) and lc_tm_at (S K) k t1; k ≤ j unchanged ✓.

open_tm_ty_rec_lc_id:
```
Lemma open_tm_ty_rec_lc_id : forall t K k j U, lc_tm_at K k t -> K <= j -> open_tm_ty_rec j U t = t.
Proof.
  induction t as ...; intros K k j U Hlc Hle; simpl.
  - reflexivity.
  - reflexivity.
  - inversion Hlc; subst. f_equal.
    + apply open_ty_rec_lc_id with (n := K); assumption.
    + apply IH1; assumption.
  - ... app: f_equal; IHs with K ✓.
  - tabs: f_equal; apply IH1 with (K := S K); [assumption | lia].
  - tapp: f_equal; [apply IH1 | apply open_ty_rec_lc_id ...]; ...
  - choice ✓.
Qed.
```

```
Lemma subst_all_term_extend : forall t sigma_ty sigma_tm x a,
  (forall Y, lc_ty_at 0 (sigma_ty Y)) ->
  (forall y, lc_tm_at 0 0 (sigma_tm y)) ->
  lc_tm_at 0 0 a ->
  ~ In x (fv_tm t) ->
  subst_all sigma_ty (fun y => if Nat.eqb y x then a else sigma_tm y)

All the infrastructure so far compiles (with the theorem still Admitted). Now the big part: the semantic relation V, R, and the lemmas.

Let me now write the semantic section:

```
(* ---------- semantic types and the logical relation ---------- *)

Definition sem : Type := tm -> Prop.

(* Lookup in the level environment (de Bruijn index -> predicate). *)
Fixpoint lvl (xi : list sem) (i : nat) : sem :=
  match xi with
  | [] => fun _ => False
  | P :: xi' => match i with
                | 0 => P
                | S j => lvl xi' j
                end
  end.

Fixpoint V (rho : atom -> sem) (xi : list sem) (T : ty) : sem :=
  match T with
  | Ty_BVar i => lvl xi i
  | Ty_FVar X => rho X
  | Ty_Arrow T1 T2 =>
      fun t => forall a,
        (exists v, multi a v /\ value v /\ V rho xi T1 v) ->
        (exists v, multi (tm_app t a) v /\ value v /\ V rho xi T2 v)
  | Ty_All T1 =>
      fun t => forall U R0,
        lc_ty_at 0 U ->
        (exists v, multi (tm_tapp t U) v /\ value v /\ V rho (R0 :: xi) T1 v)
  end.

Definition R (rho : atom -> sem) (xi : list sem) (T : ty) : sem :=
  fun t => exists v, multi t v /\ value v /\ V rho xi T v.
```

Note the Fixpoint V recurses into T1, T2 and V rho (R0::xi) T1 — structurally on T ✓. But wait: in the Ty_Arrow case, the recursive calls V rho xi T1 v and V rho xi T2 v are inside the body; the Fixpoint guard checker should accept since T1 and T2 are subterms ✓.

Also, `sem := tm -> Prop` — then rho : atom -> sem and xi : list sem ✓.

List lemmas for lvl:
```
Lemma lvl_0 : forall P xi, lvl (P :: xi) 0 = P.
Proof. reflexivity. Qed.  (* definitional? match i=0 → P ✓ *)

Lemma lvl_S : forall P xi i, lvl (P :: xi) (S i) = lvl xi i.
Proof. reflexivity. Qed.

Lemma lvl_app_lt : forall xi1 xi2 i, i < length xi1 -> lvl (xi1 ++ xi2) i = lvl xi1 i.
Proof. induction xi1; intros xi2 i H; simpl in *. 
  - inversion H.
  - destruct i. + reflexivity. + simpl. apply IHxi1. lia.
Qed.

Lemma lvl_app_ge : forall xi1 xi2 i, length xi1 <= i -> lvl (xi1 ++ xi2) i = lvl xi2 (i - length xi1).
Proof. induction xi1; intros xi2 i H; simpl in *.
  - destruct i; simpl; [reflexivity | ]. 
    Hmm, for xi1 = [], lvl xi2 i must equal lvl xi2 (i - 0) — need Nat.sub_0_r. 
  ...
Qed.
```

Now, let me think about the overall statement of the fundamental theorem and whether the induction will go through in Coq. The induction is on the derivation H : has_type Delta Gamma t T. The goal must be general over sigma_ty, rho, sigma_tm and the hypotheses. So:
```
Lemma fundamental : forall Delta Gamma t T,
  has_type Delta Gamma t T ->
  forall sigma_ty rho sigma_tm,
    (forall X, lc_ty_at 0 (sigma_ty X)) ->
    (forall y, lc_tm_at 0 0 (sigma_tm y)) ->
    (forall x V0, lookup_context x Gamma = Some V0 -> R rho [] V0 (sigma_tm x)) ->
    R rho [] T (subst_all sigma_ty sigma_tm t) /\
    lc_ty_at 0 T /\
    lc_tm_at 0 0 (subst_all sigma_ty sigma_tm t).
Proof.
  intros Delta Gamma t T H.
  induction H; intros sigma_ty rho sigma_tm Hsty Hstm Henv.
```

Lemma multi_app_l : forall t1 t1' t2,

Lemma lookup_context_eq : forall Gamma x T, lookup_context x ((x,T)::Gamma) = Some T.
Proof. intros. simpl. rewrite Nat.eqb_refl. reflexivity. Qed.

Lemma lookup_context_neq : forall Gamma x y T, x <> y -> lookup_context x ((y,T)::Gamma) = lookup_context x Gamma.
Proof. intros. simpl. destruct (Nat.eqb x y) eqn:E. apply Nat.eqb_eq in E. contradiction. reflexivity. Qed.

Let me write a helper lemma to avoid repeating this "arrow clause iff" pattern:
```
Lemma V_arrow_congr : forall (P1 P1' P2 P2' : sem),
  (forall v, P1 v <-> P1' v) -> (forall v, P2 v <-> P2' v) ->
  forall t,
    (forall a, (exists v, multi a v /\ value v /\ P1 v) ->
               (exists v, multi (tm_app t a) v /\ value v /\ P2 v)) <->
    (forall a, (exists v, multi a v /\ value v /\ P1' v) ->
               (exists v, multi (tm_app t a) v /\ value v /\ P2' v)).
Proof.
  intros P1 P1' P2 P2' H1 H2 t. split; intros H a Ha.
  - destruct Ha as (v & Hm & Hv & HV).
    assert (HV' : P1 v) by (apply (proj2 (H1 v)); assumption).
    destruct (H a (ex_intro _ v (conj Hm (conj Hv HV')))) as (v' & Hm' & Hv' & HV'').
    exists v'. split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]].
    apply (proj1 (H2 v')); assumption.
  - destruct Ha as (v & Hm & Hv & HV).
    assert (HV' : P1' v) by (apply (proj1 (H1 v)); assumption).
    destruct (H a (ex_intro _ v (conj Hm (conj Hv HV')))) as (v' & Hm' & Hv' & HV'').
    exists v'. split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]].
    apply (proj2 (H2 v')); assumption.
Qed.
```
Careful: H1 : forall v, P1 v ↔ P1' v; proj2 (H1 v) : P1' v -> P1 v ✓ (to convert a P1' hypothesis to P1). proj1: P1 → P1'.

Hmm naming conflict. Let me write:
```
Lemma V_all_congr : forall (rho : atom -> sem) T1 xi xi' t,
  (forall R0 v, V rho (R0 :: xi) T1 v <-> V rho (R0 :: xi') T1 v) ->
  forall U R0, lc_ty_at 0 U ->
    ((exists v, multi (tm_tapp t U) v /\ value v /\ V rho (R0::xi) T1 v) <->
     (exists v, multi (tm_tapp t U) v /\ value v /\ V rho (R0::xi') T1 v)).
Proof.
  intros rho T1 xi xi' t Hiff U R0 Hlc. split; intros (v & Hm & Hv & HV);
  exists v; split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]].
  - apply (proj1 (Hiff R0 v)); assumption.
  - apply (proj2 (Hiff R0 v)); assumption.
Qed.
```

  has_type Delta Gamma t T ->

    lc_tm_at 0 0 (subst_all sigma_ty sigma_tm t).

                 | L Delta Gamma T1 t2 T2 Hwf Hbody IHbody

```

**T_Abs case (detailed)**:

Context: L, Delta, Gamma, T1, t2, T2, Hwf : wf_ty Delta T1, Hbody : forall x, ~In x L -> has_type Delta (x|->T1;Gamma) (open_tm t2 (fvar x)) T2, IHbody : forall x, ~In x L -> forall sigma_ty rho sigma_tm, Hsty -> Hstm -> Henv(for extended context) -> R rho [] T2 (subst_all sigma_ty sigma_tm (open_tm t2 (fvar x))).

Goal: R rho [] (Arrow T1 T2) (tm_abs (ty_subst_all sigma_ty T1) (subst_all sigma_ty sigma_tm t2)).

Proof:
```
  - simpl.
    (* the goal is R rho [] (Arrow T1 T2) (tm_abs (ty_subst_all sigma_ty T1) (subst_all sigma_ty sigma_tm t2)) *)
    unfold R.
    exists (tm_abs (ty_subst_all sigma_ty T1) (subst_all sigma_ty sigma_tm t2)).
    split.
    + apply multi_refl.
    + split.
      * apply v_abs.
        apply lc_tm_at_subst_all.
        -- apply has_type_lc_tm with (Delta := Delta) (Gamma := Gamma) (T := Ty_Arrow T1 T2).
           apply T_Abs with (L := L); assumption.
        -- exact Hsty.
        -- exact Hstm.
      * (* V Arrow *) simpl. intros a (a' & Hma & Hva & HVa).
        (* instantiate IH at a fresh x *)
        pose (x := fresh (L ++ fv_ctx Gamma ++ fv_tm t2)).
        assert (HxL : ~ In x L).
        { unfold x. intros Hin. apply (fresh_not_in (L ++ fv_ctx Gamma ++ fv_tm t2)).
          apply in_or_app. left. apply in_or_app. left. assumption. }
        assert (Hxctx : ~ In x (fv_ctx Gamma)).
        { unfold x. intros Hin. apply (fresh_not_in ...). apply in_or_app. left. apply in_or_app. right. assumption. }
        assert (Hxt : ~ In x (fv_tm t2)).
        { unfold x. intros Hin. apply (fresh_not_in ...). apply in_or_app. right. assumption. }
        pose proof (IHbody x HxL sigma_ty rho
                      (fun y => if Nat.eqb y x then a' else sigma_tm y)
                      Hsty
                      (fun y => ...lc...) 
                      (fun y V0 Hlook => ...R...)) as HIH.
        (* HIH : R rho [] T2 (subst_all sigma_ty (fun y => if y=?x then a' else sigma_tm y) (open_tm t2 (fvar x))) *)
        (* rewrite the term *)
        rewrite (subst_all_term_extend t2 sigma_ty sigma_tm x a' Hsty Hstm Hva_lc Hxt) in HIH.
        (* HIH : R rho [] T2 (open_tm_rec 0 a' (subst_all sigma_ty sigma_tm t2)) *)
        (* from HIH get a witness w *)
        unfold R in HIH. destruct HIH as (w & Hmw & Hvw & HVw).
        exists w. split.
        -- (* multi: app (abs ...) a -->* w *)
           eapply multi_trans.
           ++ apply multi_app_r with (v1 := tm_abs (ty_subst_all sigma_ty T1) (subst_all sigma_ty sigma_tm t2)).
              ** apply v_abs. apply lc_tm_at_subst_all; [rebuild | Hsty | Hstm].
              ** exact Hma.
           ++ eapply multi_trans.
              ** apply multi_one. apply ST_AppAbs.
                 --- apply lc_tm_at_subst_all; [rebuild | Hsty | Hstm].
                 --- exact Hva.
              ** exact Hmw.
        -- split; [exact Hvw | exact HVw].
```

**T_App case**:
Context: Delta Gamma t1 t2 T1 T2 H1 IH1 H2 IH2.
Goal: R rho [] T2 (tm_app (subst_all σ t1) (subst_all σ t2)).
IH1 : R rho [] (Arrow T1 T2) (subst_all σ t1) = ∃v1, multi s1 v1 ∧ value v1 ∧ V(Arrow T1 T2) v1.
IH2 : R rho [] T1 s2.
Destruct IH1: v1, Hm1, Hv1, HV1. HV1 : forall a, R(T1) a -> R(T2)(app v1 a) (after simpl).
Apply HV1 to a := s2 with IH2 ✓: gives R(T2)(app v1 s2) = ∃w, multi (app v1 s2) w ∧ ... 
Goal: R(T2)(app s1 s2). Use the multi chain: app s1 s2 -->* app v1 s2 (multi_app_l Hm1 (lc of s2)) -->* w.
lc of s2: from has_type_lc_tm H2 and lc_tm_at_subst_all with Hsty Hstm ✓.
```
unfold R in IH1. destruct IH1 as (v1 & Hm1 & Hv1 & HV1).
simpl in HV1.
pose proof (HV1 s2 IH2) as H2rel. unfold R in H2rel. destruct H2rel as (w & Hmw & Hvw & HVw).
unfold R. exists w. split.
- eapply multi_trans.
  + apply multi_app_l; [exact Hm1 | apply lc_tm_at_subst_all; [apply has_type_lc_tm with ... H2 | exact Hsty | exact Hstm]].
  + exact Hmw.
- split; [exact Hvw | exact HVw].
```
Note: IH1 is an induction hypothesis (a forall over sigma_ty rho sigma_tm and hyps) — wait, the IH's type is the full statement: forall sigma_ty rho sigma_tm, Hsty -> Hstm -> Henv -> R ... So in the case, IH1 is universally quantified; I need to instantiate it with the current sigma_ty rho sigma_tm and hypotheses: `pose proof (IH1 sigma_ty rho sigma_tm Hsty Hstm Henv) as HR1.` ✓. Same for IH2.

**T_Choice case**:
Goal: R rho [] T (tm_choice (subst_all σ t1) (subst_all σ t2)).
IH1 : R rho [] T s1, IH2 : R rho [] T s2.
Unfold IH1 → v, Hm, Hv, HV.
lc of s1, s2: from has_type_lc_tm (T_Choice ... H1 H2) — rebuild the derivation, then lc_tm_at_subst_all.
Step: choice s1 s2 --> s1 (ST_ChoiceLeft with lc s1, lc s2) -->* v.
```
unfold R in IH1. destruct IH1 as (v & Hm & Hv & HV).
unfold R. exists v. split.
- eapply multi_trans.
  + apply multi_one. apply ST_ChoiceLeft; [lc s1 | lc s2].
  + exact Hm.
- split; [exact Hv | exact HV].
```

has_type_lc_ty:
```
Lemma has_type_lc_ty : forall Delta Gamma t T, has_type Delta Gamma t T -> lc_ty_at 0 T.
Proof.
  intros Delta Gamma t T H.
  induction H as [Delta Gamma x T Hlook Hwf
                 | L Delta Gamma T1 t2 T2 Hwf Hbody IHbody
                 | Delta Gamma t1 t2 T1 T2 H1 IH1 H2 IH2
                 | L Delta Gamma t T1 Hbody IHbody
                 | Delta Gamma t T U H1 IH1 HwfU
                 | Delta Gamma t1 t2 T H1 IH1 H2 IH2].
  - apply wf_ty_lc. assumption.
  - apply lc_ty_arrow.
    + apply wf_ty_lc. assumption.
    + pose proof (IHbody (fresh L) (fresh_not_in L)) as Hlc.
      exact Hlc.
  - inversion IH1. assumption.
  - apply lc_ty_all.
    pose proof (IHbody (fresh L) (fresh_not_in L)) as Hlc.
    (* Hlc : lc_ty_at 0 (open_ty T1 (Ty_FVar (fresh L))) *)
    apply (lc_ty_at_open_inv T1 0 (Ty_FVar (fresh L))). 
    exact Hlc.  (* need open_ty_rec 0 (FVar ...) T1, definitional ✓ *)
  - (* TApp *) apply (lc_ty_at_open T 0 U).
    + inversion IH1. assumption.
    + apply wf_ty_lc. assumption.
  - assumption.
Qed.
```
Careful: lc_ty_at_open : forall T k U, lc_ty_at (S k) T -> lc_ty_at k U -> lc_ty_at k (open_ty_rec k U T). With k=0: lc_ty_at 1 T and lc_ty_at 0 U → lc_ty_at 0 (open_ty_rec 0 U T) ✓. The goal is lc_ty_at 0 (open_ty T U) = lc_ty_at 0 (open_ty_rec 0 U T) ✓ definitional.
`apply (lc_ty_at_open T 0 U)` leaves lc_ty_at (S 0) T and lc_ty_at 0 U ✓. Inversion IH1 : lc_ty_at 0 (Ty_All T) → lc_ty_at 1 T ✓.

For TAbs: `lc_ty_at_open_inv : forall T k U, lc_ty_at k (open_ty_rec k U T) -> lc_ty_at (S k) T`. With k=0: lc_ty_at 1 T ✓ from lc_ty_at 0 (open_ty_rec 0 (FVar X) T1) ✓.

has_type_lc_tm:
```
Lemma has_type_lc_tm : forall Delta Gamma t T, has_type Delta Gamma t T -> lc_tm_at 0 0 t.
Proof.
  intros Delta Gamma t T H.
  induction H as [...same...].
  - apply lc_tm_fvar.
  - apply lc_tm_abs.
    + apply wf_ty_lc. assumption.
    + pose proof (IHbody (fresh L) (fresh_not_in L)) as Hlc.
      (* Hlc : lc_tm_at 0 0 (open_tm t2 (fvar (fresh L))) *)

      exact Hlc.

    + lia.
    + exact Hlc.
  - apply lc_tm_tapp.
    + assumption.
    + apply wf_ty_lc. assumption.
  - apply lc_tm_choice; assumption.
Qed.
```

Lemma fundamental : forall Delta Gamma t T,
  has_type Delta Gamma t T ->
  forall sigma_ty rho sigma_tm,
    (forall X, lc_ty_at 0 (sigma_ty X)) ->
    (forall y, lc_tm_at 0 0 (sigma_tm y)) ->
    (forall x V0, lookup_context x Gamma = Some V0 -> R rho [] V0 (sigma_tm x)) ->
    R rho [] T (subst_all sigma_ty sigma_tm t).
Proof.
  intros Delta Gamma t T H.
  induction H as [Delta Gamma x T Hlook Hwf
                 | L Delta Gamma T1 t2 T2 Hwf Hbody IHbody
                 | Delta Gamma t1 t2 T1 T2 H1 IH1 H2 IH2
                 | L Delta Gamma t T1 Hbody IHbody
                 | Delta Gamma t T U H1 IH1 HwfU
                 | Delta Gamma t1 t2 T H1 IH1 H2 IH2];
  intros sigma_ty rho sigma_tm Hsty Hstm Henv;
  simpl.
  - apply Henv. assumption.
  - (* T_Abs *)
    ...
```

  intros t Ht U v HwfU Hvalv HvU.
